Continuing work from thread T-e982211e-9ad0-465b-b65e-288965968077. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@docs/ORGANIZATION-CODEBASE.md @docs/ROADMAP.md @docs/feature/SPLIT_VIEW.md @docs/feature/TEXT-SHRINK-EXPAND-SELECTION.md @docs/feature/handle-file-dropping.md @docs/feature/workspace-management.md @src/main.rs @src/update.rs @src/lib.rs @src/model/mod.rs @src/model/editor_area.rs @tests/layout.rs

I just completed implementing split view for the Token text editor (all 7 phases) and added comprehensive test coverage (47 layout tests, 323 total tests).

Key implementation details for split view:
- EditorArea contains documents (HashMap by DocumentId), editors (HashMap by EditorId), groups (HashMap by GroupId), and a LayoutNode tree
- LayoutNode is either Group(GroupId) or Split(SplitContainer with direction, children, ratios)
- Documents are shared across views; each EditorGroup has tabs referencing EditorIds
- Tests in tests/layout.rs cover splits, tab operations, focus, close operations, and edge cases

I fixed two bugs discovered through testing:
- close_tab on last group's only tab now prevented (was leaving group in invalid state)
- move_tab to invalid group now no-op (was losing tabs)

Current keybindings I set up for testing:
- Numpad 1-4: Focus group 1-4 (no modifiers)
- Numpad -: Split horizontal
- Numpad +: Split vertical
- Cmd+W: Close tab
- Option+Cmd+Left/Right: Previous/Next tab
- Shift+Cmd+1-4: Focus group by index (backup)

I added physical_key parameter to handle_key() function to support numpad detection.

I created docs/ORGANIZATION-CODEBASE.md with a plan to restructure the codebase. The plan includes:
- Converting update.rs into update/ module directory with submodules: editor.rs, document.rs, layout.rs, ui.rs, app.rs
- Extracting from main.rs: view.rs (Renderer), perf.rs (PerfStats + debug overlay), app.rs (App struct + ApplicationHandler), input.rs (handle_key)
- 12 incremental migration steps

Current file sizes:
- src/main.rs: ~3000 lines
- src/update.rs: ~2600 lines
- src/model/ directory: well-organized with editor.rs, document.rs, editor_area.rs, status_bar.rs, ui.rs

Planned features from docs/ROADMAP.md:
- Expand/Shrink Selection (design doc exists at docs/feature/TEXT-SHRINK-EXPAND-SELECTION.md)
- File Dropping (design doc at docs/feature/handle-file-dropping.md)
- Workspace Management (design doc at docs/feature/workspace-management.md)

Deferred split view items:
- Cursor adjustment when other views edit same document
- Splitter drag resize (splitters render but not draggable)
- Tab drag-and-drop between groups

Established patterns:
- TDD: write failing tests first, then implement
- Use saturating_sub for arithmetic to prevent overflow panics
- Tests use test_model() helper from tests/common/mod.rs
- Elm architecture: Message → Update → Command → Render

Focus on the next steps, revise the organization codebase md file based on the recent changes and current state of the code, also provide suggested file structure for features we are planning to make in docs/feature/*

update.rs:\n\n- `pub fn update_document(...)`\n- `delete_selection`, `cursors_in_reverse_order`\n- Text insertion/deletion helpers\n- Clipboard operations\n- Multi-cursor batch edit logic\n\n### `src/update/layout.rs`\n\nMove from update.rs:\n\n- `pub fn update_layout(...)`\n- `split_focused_group`, `split_group`\n- `insert_split_in_layout`, `remove_group_from_layout`\n- `close_group`, `close_tab`, `move_tab`\n- `collect_group_ids`, `focus_adjacent_group`\n- Tab navigation (`NextTab`, `PrevTab`, `SwitchToTab`)\n\n### `src/update/ui.rs`\n\nMove from update.rs:\n\n- `pub fn update_ui(...)`\n- Status bar updates\n- Transient message handling\n- Cursor blink toggle\n\n### `src/update/app.rs`\n\nMove from update.rs:\n\n- `pub fn update_app(...)`\n- File save/load handling\n- Window resize handling (updates all editor viewports)\n\n---\n\n## Migration Plan\n\nExecute incrementally - each step should compile and pass tests.\n\n### Phase 1: Restructure update/ (1-2 hours)\n\n1. **Convert to module directory**\n   ```bash\n   mkdir -p src/update\n   git mv src/update.rs src/update/mod.rs\n   ```\n2. **Create skeleton submodules**\n\n   ```bash\n   touch src/update/{editor,document,layout,ui,app}.rs\n   ```\n\n   Add `mod` declarations to `src/update/mod.rs`\n\n3. **Move update_layout + helpers** (start here - cleanest boundaries)\n   - Cut `update_layout` and layout helpers into `layout.rs`\n   - Add imports, update `mod.rs` to call `layout::update_layout`\n   - Run tests\n\n4. **Move update_ui**\n   - Smallest handler, quick win\n\n5. **Move update_app**\n   - File I/O handlers\n\n6. **Move update_document + helpers**\n   - Text manipulation code, clipboard, multi-cursor batch edits\n\n7. **Move update_editor + cursor helpers**\n   - Largest chunk, do last\n\n### Phase 2: Extract rendering from main.rs (1-2 hours)\n\n8. **Create view.rs**\n   - Add `mod view;` to main.rs\n   - Move `Renderer` struct + impl\n   - Move `draw_text`, `draw_sparkline`\n   - Move tab expansion helpers\n   - Move rendering static functions\n\n9. **Create perf.rs**\n   - Add `mod perf;` to main.rs\n   - Move `PerfStats` + `render_perf_overlay`\n   - Keep behind `#[cfg(debug_assertions)]`\n\n### Phase 3: Extract app glue (1 hour)\n\n10. **Create app.rs (binary module)**\n    - Move `App` struct\n    - Move `ApplicationHandler` impl\n\n11. **Create input.rs**\n    - Move `handle_key`\n    - Update app.rs to use it\n\n12. **Cleanup main.rs**\n    - Should be ~100-200 lines\n    - Just CLI, EventLoop, wiring\n\n---\n\n## Feature File Structure\n\nFor planned features, follow these patterns:\n\n### Expand/Shrink Selection (docs/feature/TEXT-SHRINK-EXPAND-SELECTION.md)\n\nFiles to modify:\n```\nsrc/model/editor.rs          # Add selection_history: Vec<Selection>\nsrc/messages.rs              # Add ExpandSelection, ShrinkSelection to EditorMsg\nsrc/update/editor.rs         # Implement expand/shrink logic (after migration)\n  OR src/update.rs           # If before migration\nsrc/input.rs                 # Add Option+Up/Down keybindings (after migration)\n  OR src/main.rs             # If before migration\n```\n\n### File Dropping (docs/feature/handle-file-dropping.md)\n\nNew files:\n```\nsrc/model/drop.rs            # DropState struct\n```\n\nFiles to modify:\n```\nsrc/model/mod.rs             # Add mod drop, extend UiState\nsrc/model/ui.rs              # Add drop_state: DropState field\nsrc/messages.rs              # Add DropMsg enum, extend Msg\nsrc/update/app.rs            # Add update_drop() (after migration)\n  OR src/update.rs           # If before migration\nsrc/theme.rs                 # Add DropZoneTheme\nsrc/view.rs                  # Add render_drop_overlay() (after migration)\n  OR src/main.rs             # If before migration\nsrc/app.rs                   # Handle WindowEvent::DroppedFile/HoveredFile (after migration)\n  OR src/main.rs             # If before migration\nthemes/*.yaml                # Add drop_zone colors\n```\n\n### Workspace Management (docs/feature/workspace-management.md)\n\nNew files:\n```\nsrc/cli.rs                   # CliArgs, StartupConfig, clap integration\nsrc/model/workspace.rs       # Workspace, FileTree, FileNode structs\nsrc/fs_watcher.rs            # FileSystemWatcher using notify crate\n```\n\nFiles to modify:\n```\nsrc/model/mod.rs             # Add mod workspace, extend AppModel\nsrc/messages.rs              # Add WorkspaceMsg enum\nsrc/update/workspace.rs      # NEW: update_workspace() handler\nsrc/update/mod.rs            # Add workspace module dispatch\nsrc/theme.rs                 # Add SidebarTheme, FileTreeTheme\nsrc/view.rs                  # Add render_sidebar(), render_file_tree() (after migration)\n  OR src/main.rs             # If before migration\nsrc/input.rs                 # Add Cmd+B, arrow keys for tree (after migration)\n  OR src/main.rs             # If before migration\nCargo.toml                   # Add clap, notify dependencies\n```\n\n---\n\n## Guidelines\n\n### When adding new code:\n\n- **Model changes** → `src/model/` appropriate file\n- **New message type** → `src/messages.rs` + handler in `src/update/` appropriate file\n- **New keyboard shortcut** → `src/input.rs` (or `src/main.rs` if before migration)\n- **New rendering logic** → `src/view.rs` (or `src/main.rs` if before migration)\n- **New UI component** → consider if it's model (state) or view (rendering)\n\n### When a file gets too large:\n\n- `update/editor.rs` > 800 lines → consider `update/editor/cursor.rs`, `update/editor/selection.rs`\n- `view.rs` > 1200 lines → consider `view/` module directory with `view/editor.rs`, `view/tabs.rs`\n- `model/editor_area.rs` > 1000 lines → consider `model/layout.rs` for LayoutNode/SplitContainer\n\n### Cross-module dependencies:\n\n- Update submodules should NOT call each other directly\n- All cross-domain routing goes through the main `update` dispatcher\n- Shared helpers used by multiple update modules → keep in `update/mod.rs` or move to `model/`\n\n---\n\n## File Ownership Summary\n\n| Concern               | Files                                       |\n| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |\n| **State/Model**       | `model/*.rs`                                |\n| **Messages**          | `messages.rs`                               |\n| **State Updates**     | `update/*.rs`                               |\n| **Rendering**         | `view.rs`, `perf.rs`, `overlay.rs`          |\n| **Input Handling**    | `input.rs`                                  |\n| **Winit Integration** | `app.rs` (binary), `main.rs`                |\n| **Theming**           | `theme.rs`                                  |\n| **Commands**          | `commands.rs`                               |\n| **Utilities**         | `util.rs`                                   |\n| **CLI/Startup**       | `cli.rs` (future)                           |\n| **File Watching**     | `fs_watcher.rs` (future)                    |\n\n---\n\n## Not Doing (Yet)\n\nThese are deferred until actually needed:\n\n- **Separate crates** (`core`, `gui`) - Only if we add another frontend (TUI, web)\n- **Trait abstractions** for rendering - YAGNI for single frontend\n- **Plugin system** - Wait for stable feature set first\n- **Async runtime** - Not needed for current sync file I/O\n\n---\n\n## Deferred Split View Items\n\nThese items were identified during split view implementation but deferred:\n\n- **Cursor adjustment when other views edit same document** - Requires notification system\n- **Splitter drag resize** - Splitters render but not draggable yet\n- **Tab drag-and-drop between groups** - Low priority, keyboard shortcuts work\n\n---\n\n## Current Keybindings (Split View)\n\n| Shortcut               | Action                  |\n| ---------------------- | ----------------------- |\n| Numpad 1-4             | Focus group 1-4         |\n| Numpad -               | Split horizontal        |\n| Numpad +               | Split vertical          |\n| Cmd+W                  | Close tab               |\n| Option+Cmd+Left/Right  | Previous/Next tab       |\n| Shift+Cmd+1-4          | Focus group by index    |\n| Ctrl+Tab               | Focus next group        |\n"
}

  "diff": "```diff\nIndex: ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\n===================================================================\n--- ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\toriginal\n+++ ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\tmodified\n@@ -3,8 +3,106 @@\n All notable changes to rust-editor are documented in this file.\n \n ---\n \n+## 2025-12-06 (Latest)\n+\n+### Added - Split View Implementation (All 7 Phases)\n+\n+Complete multi-pane editor with split views, tabs, and shared documents.\n+\n+#### Phase 1: Core Data Structures\n+- `DocumentId`, `EditorId`, `GroupId`, `TabId` - typed identifiers\n+- `Tab` struct with editor reference, pinned/preview flags\n+- `EditorGroup` with tabs, active tab index, layout rect\n+- `LayoutNode` enum: `Group(GroupId)` or `Split(SplitContainer)`\n+- `SplitContainer` with direction, children, ratios, min_sizes\n+- `EditorArea` managing documents, editors, groups, and layout tree\n+\n+#### Phase 2: Layout System\n+- `Rect` type for layout calculations with `contains()` hit testing\n+- `compute_layout()` recursive algorithm for layout tree\n+- `group_at_point()` for mouse hit testing\n+- `SplitterBar` struct for splitter positions\n+- `splitter_at_point()` for resize handle detection\n+- `SPLITTER_WIDTH` constant (4px)\n+\n+#### Phase 3: AppModel Migration\n+- Replaced single `Document`/`EditorState` with `EditorArea`\n+- Backward-compatible accessor methods: `document()`, `editor()`, etc.\n+- `ensure_focused_cursor_visible()` helper avoiding document cloning\n+- `resize()` now updates ALL editors (fixes multi-pane viewport bug)\n+\n+#### Phase 4: LayoutMsg Handlers\n+- `SplitFocused(SplitDirection)` - split current group\n+- `SplitGroup { group_id, direction }` - split specific group\n+- `CloseGroup`, `CloseFocusedGroup` - close with layout cleanup\n+- `FocusGroup`, `FocusNextGroup`, `FocusPrevGroup` - navigation\n+- `FocusGroupByIndex(usize)` - keyboard shortcuts (1-indexed)\n+- `CloseTab`, `CloseFocusedTab`, `MoveTab` - tab operations\n+- `NextTab`, `PrevTab`, `SwitchToTab` - tab navigation\n+\n+#### Phase 5: Multi-Group Rendering\n+- `render_all_groups_static()` iterates over layout\n+- `render_editor_group_static()` renders single pane\n+- Tab bar rendering with active/inactive styling\n+- Splitter bar rendering between groups\n+- Focus indicator (border) on focused group\n+\n+#### Phase 6: Document Synchronization\n+- Documents shared across views (same `DocumentId`)\n+- Independent cursor/viewport per `EditorState`\n+- Edits reflect immediately in all views of same document\n+\n+#### Phase 7: Keyboard Shortcuts\n+- Numpad 1-4: Focus group by index\n+- Numpad -/+: Split horizontal/vertical\n+- Cmd+W: Close tab\n+- Option+Cmd+Left/Right: Previous/Next tab\n+- Ctrl+Tab: Focus next group\n+- `physical_key` support in `handle_key()` for numpad detection\n+\n+### Fixed - Split View Bugs\n+\n+- `close_tab` on last group's only tab now prevented (was leaving invalid state)\n+- `move_tab` to invalid group now no-op (was losing tabs)\n+- Viewport resize updates all editors, not just focused one\n+\n+### Added - Performance Overlay Sparklines\n+\n+- Historical sparkline charts for render timing breakdown\n+- 60-frame rolling history per metric (clear, highlight, gutter, text, cursor, status, present)\n+- `draw_sparkline()` function with 1px bar visualization\n+- `record_render_history()` pushes timing to VecDeque histories\n+\n+### Added - Multi-Cursor Batch Undo/Redo\n+\n+- `EditOperation::Batch` for atomic multi-cursor operations\n+- InsertChar, InsertNewline, DeleteBackward, DeleteForward now batch\n+- Proper cursor restoration on undo/redo\n+- 6 new tests for multi-cursor undo behavior\n+\n+### Added - SelectAllOccurrences (Cmd+Shift+L)\n+\n+- Finds all occurrences of word/selection in document\n+- Creates cursor+selection for each occurrence\n+- Status message shows count: \"Selected N occurrences\"\n+\n+### Added - Layout Tests\n+\n+- 47 new tests in `tests/layout.rs`\n+- Split operations, close operations, focus navigation\n+- Tab operations (close, move, switch)\n+- Independent viewport/cursor per editor\n+- Edge cases (nested splits, invalid IDs)\n+\n+### Changed\n+\n+- Test count: 351 (was 246)\n+- Added 47 layout tests, 6 multi-cursor undo tests, selection tests\n+\n+---\n+\n ## 2025-12-06\n \n ### Added - Caret Count in Status Bar\n \n```",

Overview\n8: \n9: ### Current State\n10: \n11: The codebase already has foundational types in `model/editor.rs`:\n12: \n13: ```rust\n14: // Already implemented\n15: pub struct Position { pub line: usize, pub column: usize }\n16: pub struct Selection { pub anchor: Position, pub head: Position }\n17: pub struct Cursor { pub line: usize, pub column: usize, pub desired_column: Option<usize> }\n18: pub struct EditorState {\n19:     pub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,\n20:     pub selections: Vec<Selection>,  // Parallel to cursors\n21:     // ...\n22: }\n23: ```\n24: \n25: **What's missing:**\n26: \n27: - Selection movement (Shift+Arrow)\n28: - Selection rendering\n29: - Multi-cursor editing logic\n30: - Rectangle selection\n31: - Clipboard operations with selections\n32: - Word/line selection\n33: \n34: ### Goals\n35: \n36: | Feature              | Description                                            |\n37: | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |\n38: | **Text Selection**   | Shift+movement extends selection from anchor           |\n39: | **Multi-Cursor**     | Multiple independent cursors editing simultaneously    |\n40: | **Rectangle Select** | Column-mode selection via middle mouse drag            |\n41: | **Clone Cursor**     | Add cursor above/below via Option+Option+Arrow         |\n42: | **Clipboard**        | Cut/copy/paste respecting selections                   |\n43: | **Word/Line Select** | Double-click word, triple-click line, Cmd+D next match |\n44: \n45: ---\n46: \n47: ## Part 1: Selection Model\n48: \n49: ### Selection Semantics\n50: \n51: A selection has two key positions:\n52: \n53: - **Anchor**: Where the selection started (fixed)\n54: - **Head**: Where the cursor currently is (moves with arrow keys)\n55: \n56: ```\n57: Forward selection (head after anchor):\n58:     anchor          head\n59:        │              │\n60:        ▼              ▼\n61:        Hello, World!\n62:        └──────────────┘\n63:          selected text\n64: \n65: Backward selection (head before anchor):\n66:        head          anchor\n67:        │              │\n68:        ▼              ▼\n69:        Hello, World!\n70:        └──────────────┘\n71:          selected text\n72: ```\n73: \n74: **Direction preservation** is critical:\n75: \n76: - Shift+Right with forward selection: head moves right\n77: - Shift+Left with forward selection: head moves left (may cross anchor)\n78: - When head crosses anchor, selection reverses direction\n79: \n80: ### Selection State\n81: \n82: Each cursor has a parallel selection:\n83: \n84: ```rust\n85: // In EditorState\n86: pub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,      // Cursor positions\n87: pub selections: Vec<Selection>, // Parallel selections (cursors[i] pairs with selections[i])\n88: \n89: // Invariant: cursors.len() == selections.len()\n90: // Invariant: cursors[i].to_position() == selections[i].head\n91: ```\n92: \n93: ### Selection Operations\n94: \n95: ```rust\n96: impl Selection {\n97:     /// Extend selection by moving head to new position\n98:     pub fn extend_to(&mut self, new_head: Position) {\n99:         self.head = new_head;\n100:     }\n101: \n102:     /// Collapse selection to cursor position (clear selection)\n103:     pub fn collapse_to_head(&mut self) {\n104:         self.anchor = self.head;\n105:     }\n106: \n107:     /// Collapse to anchor (used when pressing left with selection)\n108:     pub fn collapse_to_start(&mut self) {\n109:         let start = self.start();\n110:         self.anchor = start;\n111:         self.head = start;\n112:     }\n113: \n114:     /// Collapse to end (used when pressing right with selection)\n115:     pub fn collapse_to_end(&mut self) {\n116:         let end = self.end();\n117:         self.anchor = end;\n118:         self.head = end;\n119:     }\n120: \n121:     /// Get selected text from document\n122:     pub fn get_text(&self, document: &Document) -> String {\n123:         if self.is_empty() {\n124:             return String::new();\n125:         }\n126:         let start = self.start();\n127:         let end = self.end();\n128:         let start_offset = document.cursor_to_offset(start.line, start.column);\n129:         let end_offset = document.cursor_to_offset(end.line, end.column);\n130:         document.buffer.slice(start_offset..end_offset).to_string()\n131:     }\n132: \n133:     /// Check if position is within selection\n134:     pub fn contains(&self, pos: Position) -> bool {\n135:         pos >= self.start() && pos < self.end()\n136:     }\n137: }\n138: ```\n139: \n140: ---\n141: \n142: ## Part 2: Keyboard Shortcuts\n143: \n144: ### Selection Movement\n145: \n146: | Shortcut    | Action                 | Message                                         |\n147: | ----------- | ---------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |\n148: | Shift+←     | Extend selection left  | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Left)`      |\n149: | Shift+→     | Extend selection right | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Right)`     |\n150: | Shift+↑     | Extend selection up    | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Up)`        |\n151: | Shift+↓     | Extend selection down  | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Down)`      |\n152: | Shift+Home  | Extend to line start   | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection`   |\n153: | Shift+End   | Extend to line end     | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection`     |\n154: | Shift+Cmd+← | Extend word left       | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Left)`  |\n155: | Shift+Cmd+→ | Extend word right      | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Right)` |\n156: | Cmd+A       | Select all             | `EditorMsg::SelectAll`                          |\n157: | Escape      | Clear selection        | `EditorMsg::ClearSelection`                     |\n158: \n159: ### Movement with Selection\n160: \n161: When moving **without** Shift and a selection exists:\n162: \n163: - **Left/Up**: Collapse to selection start, then move\n164: - **Right/Down**: Collapse to selection end, then move\n165: \n166: ```rust\n167: fn move_cursor_left(model: &mut AppModel, extend_selection: bool) {\n168:     let selection = model.editor.selection_mut();\n169: \n170:     if extend_selection {\n171:         // Move head left, anchor stays\n172:         let new_pos = compute_position_left(selection.head, &model.document);\n173:         selection.head = new_pos;\n174:         model.editor.cursor_mut().line = new_pos.line;\n175:         model.editor.cursor_mut().column = new_pos.column;\n176:     } else if !selection.is_empty() {\n177:         // Collapse to start\n178:         let start = selection.start();\n179:         selection.collapse_to_start();\n180:         model.editor.cursor_mut().line = start.line;\n181:         model.editor.cursor_mut().column = start.column;\n182:     } else {\n183:         // Normal movement\n184:         move_cursor_left_impl(model);\n185:         selection.collapse_to_head();\n186:     }\n187: }\n188: ```\n189: \n190: ### Multi-Cursor Shortcuts\n191: \n192: | Shortcut        | Action                                   | Message                                              |\n193: | --------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |\n194: | Cmd+Click       | Toggle cursor at position                | `EditorMsg::ToggleCursorAtPosition { line, column }` |\n195: | Cmd+D           | Select next occurrence of word/selection | `EditorMsg::SelectNextOccurrence`                    |\n196: | Cmd+Shift+L     | Select all occurrences                   | `EditorMsg::SelectAllOccurrences`                    |\n197: | Option+Option+↑ | Add cursor above                         | `EditorMsg::AddCursorAbove`                          |\n198: | Option+Option+↓ | Add cursor below                         | `EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow`                          |\n199: | Escape          | Collapse to single cursor                | `EditorMsg::CollapseToSingleCursor`                  |\n200: \n201: ### Word & Line Selection\n202: \n203: | Action           | Trigger           | Behavior                                 |\n204: | ---------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- |\n205: | Select word      | Double-click      | Select word under cursor                 |\n206: | Select line      | Triple-click      | Select entire line including newline     |\n207: | Expand selection | Cmd+W (JetBrains) | Expand: word → quotes → brackets → block |\n208: \n209: ---\n210: \n211: ## Part 3: Messages\n212: \n213: ```rust\n214: // In messages.rs\n215: \n216: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n217: pub enum EditorMsg {\n218:     // Existing movement\n219:     MoveCursor(Direction),\n220:     MoveCursorLineStart,\n221:     MoveCursorLineEnd,\n222:     MoveCursorDocumentStart,\n223:     MoveCursorDocumentEnd,\n224:     MoveCursorWord(Direction),\n225:     PageUp,\n226:     PageDown,\n227:     SetCursorPosition { line: usize, column: usize },\n228:     Scroll(i32),\n229:     ScrollHorizontal(i32),\n230: \n231:     // === NEW: Selection Movement ===\n232:     MoveCursorWithSelection(Direction),\n233:     MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection,\n234:     MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection,\n235:     MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection,\n236:     MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection,\n237:     MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Direction),\n238:     PageUpWithSelection,\n239:     PageDownWithSelection,\n240: \n241:     // === NEW: Selection Commands ===\n242:     SelectAll,\n243:     SelectWord,                          // Select word at cursor\n244:     SelectLine,                          // Select entire line\n245:     ExtendSelectionToPosition { line: usize, column: usize },  // Shift+Click\n246:     ClearSelection,                      // Collapse all selections\n247: \n248:     // === NEW: Multi-Cursor ===\n249:     ToggleCursorAtPosition { line: usize, column: usize },  // Cmd+Click\n250:     AddCursorAbove,                      // Option+Option+Up\n251:     AddCursorBelow,                      // Option+Option+Down\n252:     CollapseToSingleCursor,              // Escape with multiple cursors\n253:     RemoveCursor(usize),                 // Remove cursor by index\n254: \n255:     // === NEW: Occurrence Selection (JetBrains-style) ===\n256:     AddSelectionForNextOccurrence,       // Cmd+J\n257:     UnselectOccurrence,                  // Shift+Cmd+J\n258: \n259:     // === NEW: Rectangle Selection ===\n260:     StartRectangleSelection { line: usize, column: usize },\n261:     UpdateRectangleSelection { line: usize, column: usize },\n262:     FinishRectangleSelection,\n263:     CancelRectangleSelection,\n264: }\n265: ```\n266: \n267: ---\n268: \n269: ## Part 4: Multi-Cursor Editing\n270: \n271: ### Cursor Ordering\n272: \n273: Cursors must be processed in **reverse document order** (highest offset first) to prevent edits from shifting subsequent cursor positions.\n274: \n275: ```rust\n276: /// Get cursor indices sorted by document offset descending\n277: fn cursors_in_edit_order(editor: &EditorState, document: &Document) -> Vec<usize> {\n278:     let mut indices: Vec<usize> = (0..editor.cursors.len()).collect();\n279:     indices.sort_by(|&a, &b| {\n280:         let offset_a = document.cursor_to_offset(\n281:             editor.cursors[a].line,\n282:             editor.cursors[a].column\n283:         );\n284:         let offset_b = document.cursor_to_offset(\n285:             editor.cursors[b].line,\n286:             editor.cursors[b].column\n287:         );\n288:         offset_b.cmp(&offset_a)  // Descending\n289:     });\n290:     indices\n291: }\n292: ```\n293: \n294: ### Insert Character at All Cursors\n295: \n296: ```rust\n297: fn insert_char_multi(model: &mut AppModel, ch: char) {\n298:     let indices = cursors_in_edit_order(&model.editor, &model.document);\n299: \n300:     for &i in &indices {\n301:         let cursor = &model.editor.cursors[i];\n302:         let selection = &model.editor.selections[i];\n303: \n304:         // If selection exists, delete it first\n305:         if !selection.is_empty() {\n306:             delete_selection(model, i);\n307:         }\n308: \n309:         // Insert character\n310:         let pos = model.document.cursor_to_offset(cursor.line, cursor.column);\n311:         model.document.buffer.insert_char(pos, ch);\n312: \n313:         // Move cursor right\n314:         model.editor.cursors[i].column += 1;\n315:         model.editor.selections[i] = Selection::new(\n316:             model.editor.cursors[i].to_position()\n317:         );\n318:     }\n319: \n320:     // Record undo operation (combined for all cursors)\n321:     // ...\n322: \n323:     deduplicate_cursors(model);\n324: }\n325: ```\n326: \n327: ### Cursor Deduplication\n328: \n329: After operations, cursors may overlap. Remove duplicates while preserving primary cursor (index 0).\n330: \n331: ```rust\n332: fn deduplicate_cursors(model: &mut AppModel) {\n333:     let mut seen: HashSet<(usize, usize)> = HashSet::new();\n334:     let mut to_remove: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();\n335: \n336:     // Always keep primary cursor\n337:     seen.insert((model.editor.cursors[0].line, model.editor.cursors[0].column));\n338: \n339:     for i in 1..model.editor.cursors.len() {\n340:         let key = (model.editor.cursors[i].line, model.editor.cursors[i].column);\n341:         if seen.contains(&key) {\n342:             to_remove.push(i);\n343:         } else {\n344:             seen.insert(key);\n345:         }\n346:     }\n347: \n348:     // Remove in reverse order to preserve indices\n349:     for i in to_remove.into_iter().rev() {\n350:         model.editor.cursors.remove(i);\n351:         model.editor.selections.remove(i);\n352:     }\n353: }\n354: ```\n355: \n356: ### Selection Merging\n357: \n358: When selections overlap, merge them:\n359: \n360: ```rust\n361: fn merge_overlapping_selections(editor: &mut EditorState) {\n362:     if editor.selections.len() <= 1 {\n363:         return;\n364:     }\n365: \n366:     // Sort by start position\n367:     let mut indexed: Vec<(usize, Selection)> = editor.selections\n368:         .iter()\n369:         .cloned()\n370:         .enumerate()\n371:         .collect();\n372:     indexed.sort_by_key(|(_, s)| s.start());\n373: \n374:     let mut merged: Vec<(usize, Selection)> = Vec::new();\n375: \n376:     for (i, sel) in indexed {\n377:         if let Some((_, last)) = merged.last_mut() {\n378:             if sel.start() <= last.end() {\n379:                 // Overlapping - extend last selection\n380:                 if sel.end() > last.end() {\n381:                     last.head = sel.end();  // Extend to cover both\n382:                 }\n383:                 // Remove the cursor for this merged selection\n384:                 // (handled separately)\n385:                 continue;\n386:             }\n387:         }\n388:         merged.push((i, sel));\n389:     }\n390: \n391:     // Rebuild cursors and selections from merged set\n392:     // Keep only cursors whose selections weren't merged away\n393:     // ...\n394: }\n395: ```\n396: \n397: ---\n398: \n399: ## Part 5: Rectangle Selection\n400: \n401: ### State\n402: \n403: ```rust\n404: // In EditorState\n405: pub struct EditorState {\n406:     // ... existing fields\n407: \n408:     /// Rectangle selection state (None when not in rectangle mode)\n409:     pub rectangle_selection: Option<RectangleSelectionState>,\n410: }\n411: \n412: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\n413: pub struct RectangleSelectionState {\n414:     /// Starting corner of rectangle\n415:     pub anchor: Position,\n416:     /// Current corner (follows mouse)\n417:     pub current: Position,\n418: }\n419: ```\n420: \n421: ### Algorithm\n422: \n423: ```rust\n424: fn update_rectangle_selection(model: &mut AppModel, current: Position) {\n425:     let Some(rect_state) = &model.editor.rectangle_selection else {\n426:         return;\n427:     };\n428: \n429:     let anchor = rect_state.anchor;\n430: \n431:     // Compute rectangle bounds\n432:     let start_line = anchor.line.min(current.line);\n433:     let end_line = anchor.line.max(current.line);\n434:     let start_col = anchor.column.min(current.column);\n435:     let end_col = anchor.column.max(current.column);\n436: \n437:     // Clear existing cursors/selections\n438:     model.editor.cursors.clear();\n439:     model.editor.selections.clear();\n440: \n441:     // Create cursor/selection on each line within bounds\n442:     for line in start_line..=end_line {\n443:         let line_len = model.document.line_length(line);\n444: \n445:         // Clamp columns to line length\n446:         let clamped_start = start_col.min(line_len);\n447:         let clamped_end = end_col.min(line_len);\n448: \n449:         // Cursor at the \"current\" side of rectangle\n450:         let cursor_col = if current.column > anchor.column {\n451:             clamped_end\n452:         } else {\n453:             clamped_start\n454:         };\n455: \n456:         model.editor.cursors.push(Cursor::at(line, cursor_col));\n457: \n458:         // Selection covers the rectangle width (or empty if zero-width)\n459:         if clamped_start != clamped_end {\n460:             model.editor.selections.push(Selection::from_anchor_head(\n461:                 Position::new(line, clamped_start),\n462:                 Position::new(line, clamped_end),\n463:             ));\n464:         } else {\n465:             // Zero-width: empty selection (just cursor)\n466:             model.editor.selections.push(Selection::new(\n467:                 Position::new(line, cursor_col)\n468:             ));\n469:         }\n470:     }\n471: \n472:     // Update the rectangle state\n473:     model.editor.rectangle_selection = Some(RectangleSelectionState {\n474:         anchor,\n475:         current,\n476:     });\n477: }\n478: ```\n479: \n480: ### Visual Feedback\n481: \n482: During rectangle selection, render:\n483: \n484: 1. is_in_add_cursor_mode(&self) -> bool {\n502:         self.is_held\n503:     }\n504: }\n505: ```\n506: \n507: ### Integration\n508: \n509: ```rust\n510: // In handle_key\n511: if }\n666: \n667:         let start = selection.start();\n668:         let end = selection.end();\n669: \n670:         // For each line in selection\n671:         for line in start.line..=end.line {\n672:             // Skip if line not in viewport\n673:             if line < viewport.top_line || line >= viewport.top_line + viewport.visible_lines {\n674:                 continue;\n675: == end.line { end.column } else { line_len };\n685: \n686:             // Adjust for horizontal scroll\n687:             let visible_start = sel_start_col.saturating_sub(viewport.left_column);\n688: \n694:             let x1 = text_start_x + (visible_start as f32 * char_width) as usize;\n695:             let x2 = text_start_x + (visible_end as f32 * char_width) as usize;\n696: \n697:             // Fill selection rectangle\n698:             fill_rect(buffer, x1, y, x2 - x1, line_height, selection_bg);\n699:         }\n700:     }\n701: }\n702: ```\n703: \n704: ### Multi-Cursor Rendering\n705: \n706: ```rust\n707: fn render_cursors(\n708:     buffer: &mut [u32],\n709:     model: &AppModel,\n710:     viewport: &Viewport,\n711:     line_height: usize,\n712:     char_width: f32,\n713: visible\n272:     pub viewport: Viewport,\n273:     /// Number of lines of padding to maintain above/below cursor when scrolling\n274:     pub scroll_padding: usize,\n275:     /// Rectangle selection state (for middle mouse drag)\n276:     pub rectangle_selection: RectangleSelectionState,\n277:     /// Occurrence selection state (for Cmd+J \"select next occurrence\")\n278:     pub occurrence_state: Option<OccurrenceState>,\n279: }\n280: \n281: impl EditorState {\n282:     /// Create a new editor state with default settings\n283:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n284:         let cursors: vec![cursor],\n290:             selections: vec![selection],\n291:             viewport: Viewport::default(),\n292:             scroll_padding: 1, // JetBrains-style default\n293:             rectangle_selection: RectangleSelectionState::default(),\n294:             occurrence_state: None,\n295:         }\n296:     }\n297: \n298:     /// Create an editor state with specific viewport dimensions\n299:     pub fn with_viewport(visible_lines: usize, visible_columns: usize) -> Self {\n300:         let cursor = Cursor::new();\n301:         let selection = Selection::new(cursor.to_position());\n302:         Self {\n303:             id: None,\n304:             document_id: None,\n305:             cursors: vec![cursor],\n306:             selections: vec![selection],\n307:             viewport: Viewport::new(visible_lines, visible_columns),\n308:             scroll_padding: 1,\n309:             rectangle_selection: RectangleSelectionState::default(),\n310:             occurrence_state: None,\n311:         }\n312:     }\n313: \n314:     /// Get the primary cursor (read-only)\n315:     #[inline]\n316:     pub fn cursor(&self) -> &Cursor {\n317:         &self.cursors[0]\n318:     }\n319: \n320:     /// Get the primary cursor (mutable)\n321:     #[inline]\n322:     pub fn cursor_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Cursor {\n323:         &mut self.cursors[0]\n324:     }\n325: \n326:     /// Get the primary selection (read-only)\n327:     #[inline]\n328:     pub fn selection(&self) -> &Selection {\n329: self.selections[0]\n336:     }\n337: \n338:     /// Check if there are multiple cursors\n339:     pub fn has_multiple_cursors(&self) -> bool {\n340:         self.cursors.len() > 1\n341:     }\n342: \n343:     /// Get the number of cursors\n344:     pub fn cursor_count(&self) -> usize {\n345:         self.cursors.len()\n346:     }\n347: \n348:     /// Collapse all cursors to just the primary cursor\n349:     pub fn collapse_to_primary(&mut self) {\n350:         self.cursors.truncate(1);\n351:         self.selections.truncate(1);\n352:     }\n353: \n354:     /// Update the primary selection to match the primary cursor (for non-selection moves)\n355:     pub fn clear_selection(&mut self) {\n356:         let pos = self.cursors[0].to_position();\n357:         self.selections[0] = movements to maintain invariants.\n362:     pub fn collapse_selections_to_cursors(&mut self) {\n363:         for (cursor, selection) in self.cursors.iter().zip(self.selections.iter_mut()) {\n364:             let pos = cursor.to_position();\n365:             selection.anchor = pos;\n366:             selection.head = pos;\n367:         }\n368:     }\n369: \n370:     /// Toggle a cursor at the given position\n371:     /// If a cursor exists at that position, remove it (unless it's the only one)\n372:     /// If no cursor exists there, add one\n373:     /// Returns true if a cursor was added, self\n377:             .cursors\n378:             .iter()\n379:             .position(|c| c.line == line && c.column == column);\n380: \n381:         if let Some(idx) = existing_idx {\n382: self, document: &Document, mode: ScrollRevealMode) {\n483:         let cursor = &self.cursors[0];\n484:         let padding = self.scroll_padding;\n485:         let total_lines = model.editor_mut().cursor_mut().desired_column = None;\n+    } else {\n+        // No history - collapse selection to cursor position\n+        model.editor_mut().clear_selection();\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+/// Check if selection exactly covers a word boundary\n+fn is_word_selection(selection: &Selection, model: &AppModel) -> bool {\n+    if selection.start().line != selection.end().line {\n+ Tests for expand/shrink selection feature\n\nmod common;\n\nuse common::test_model;\nuse token::messages::{EditorMsg, Msg};\nuse token::update::update;\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Expand Selection Tests\n// ============================================================================\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_from_cursor_selects_word() {\n    // Cursor in middle of \"hello\"\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Should select \"hello\" (columns 0-5)\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().column, 5);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_from_word_selects_line() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // First expand: cursor → word\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Second expand: word → line\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Should select entire line (including newline)\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().line, 1);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().column, 0);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_from_line_selects_all() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello\\nworld\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // Expand three times: cursor → word → line → all\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Should select entire document\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().line, 1);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().column, 6); // \"world\\n\".len() = 6\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_already_all_does_nothing() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello\\nworld\\n\", 0, 0);\n\n    // Expand to all\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    let sel_before = model.editor().selection().clone();\n\n    // Expand again - should not change\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    let sel_after = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel_before.start(), sel_after.start());\n    assert_eq!(sel_before.end(), sel_after.end());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_on_whitespace() {\n    // Cursor on space between words\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 5);\n\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Cursor is at end of \"hello\", so word_under_cursor may not find a word\n    // Should expand to line instead\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_on_empty_line() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello\\n\\nworld\\n\", 1, 0);\n\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Should select the empty line (just the newline)\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().line, 1);\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().line, 2);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().column, 0);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Shrink Selection Tests\n// ============================================================================\n\n#[test]\nfn test_shrink_restores_previous_selection() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // Expand: cursor → word\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Shrink: word → cursor\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n\n    // Should be back to empty selection at original position\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert!(sel.is_empty());\n    assert_eq!(model.editor().cursor().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(model.editor().cursor().column, 2);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_shrink_from_word_to_cursor() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // Expand to word\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Shrink to cursor\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert!(sel.is_empty());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_shrink_with_empty_history_clears_selection() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 0);\n\n    // Manually set a selection (simulating user made selection some other way)\n    model.editor_mut().selection_mut().anchor = token::model::Position::new(0, 0);\n    model.editor_mut().selection_mut().head = token::model::Position::new(0, 5);\n    model.editor_mut().cursor_mut().column = 5;\n\n    // History is empty, so shrink should collapse to cursor\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert!(sel.is_empty());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_then_shrink_round_trip() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\nline two\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // Expand three times: cursor → word → line → all\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Shrink three times: all → line → word → cursor\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n\n    // Should be back to original cursor position with no selection\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert!(sel.is_empty());\n    assert_eq!(model.editor().cursor().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(model.editor().cursor().column, 2);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// History Management Tests\n// ============================================================================\n\n#[test]\nfn test_history_cleared_on_cursor_movement() {\n    use token::messages::Direction;\n\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // Expand to word\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Move cursor (clears history)\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::MoveCursor(Direction::Right)));\n\n    // Shrink should now just clear selection (no history to restore)\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n\n    // Selection should be empty, cursor at moved position\n    assert!(model.editor().selection().is_empty());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_history_cleared_on_select_all() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // Expand to word\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    assert!(!model.editor().selection_history.is_empty());\n\n    // SelectAll (clears expand/shrink history)\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::SelectAll));\n\n    // History should be cleared\n    assert!(model.editor().selection_history.is_empty());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_history_preserved_during_expand_shrink_sequence() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 2);\n\n    // Expand twice\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // History should have 2 entries (original cursor, word selection)\n    assert_eq!(model.editor().selection_history.len(), 2);\n\n    // Shrink once\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n\n    // History should have 1 entry\n    assert_eq!(model.editor().selection_history.len(), 1);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Edge Cases\n// ============================================================================\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_at_word_boundary() {\n    // Cursor at exact end of word (before space)\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello world\\n\", 0, 5);\n\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // At position 5 (the space), there's no word, so should expand to line\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert!(!sel.is_empty());\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_on_single_char_word() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"a b c\\n\", 0, 0);\n\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Should select just \"a\"\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().column, 1);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_on_underscore_word() {\n    // Underscore should be treated as word char\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello_world test\\n\", 0, 3);\n\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Should select \"hello_world\" (columns 0-11)\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.end().column, 11);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_expand_empty_document() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"\", 0, 0);\n\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ExpandSelection));\n\n    // Should handle gracefully - selection at (0,0)\n    let sel = model.editor().selection();\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().line, 0);\n    assert_eq!(sel.start().column, 0);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn test_shrink_empty_history_no_crash() {\n    let mut model = test_model(\"hello\\n\", 0, 0);\n\n    // Shrink with no history should not crash\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::ShrinkSelection));\n\n    // Should just have empty selection at cursor\n    assert!(model.editor().selection().is_empty());\n}\n"

no line adjustment for same-line positions\n+            }\n+        }\n+        // Before edit column on same line - no adjustment\n+    } else {\n+        // Position is on a line after the edit line\n+        if lines_delta >= 0 {\n+            *pos_line = pos_line.saturating_add(lines_delta as usize);\n+        } else {\n+            *pos_line = pos_line.saturating_sub((-lines_delta) as usize);\n+        }\n+    }\n+}\n+\n /// Represents a draggable splitter bar between editor groups\n #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\n pub struct SplitterBar {\n     /// Direction of the split this bar controls\n```",
